# ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.pde.runtime/icons/obj16/package_obj@2x.png) cs
CompositeStructure aims at defining the common component approach composite structure pattern language (close to the UML Composite structure).

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *BlockArchitecture*

Parent class for deriving specific architectures for each design phase

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [Namespace](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namespace),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

|*system*|![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [Component](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-cs#-component)|0..1|[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.equinox.p2.ui.discovery/icons/obj16/message_info.png)](#system "The system component of the architecture block.") [![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.debug.ui/icons/full/obj16/read_obj.png)](#system "pattern BlockArchitecture__system(self : BlockArchitecture, target : Component) { 	find SystemAnalysis__system(self, target); } or { 	find LogicalArchitecture__system(self, target); } or { 	find PhysicalArchitecture__system(self, target); } or { 	find EPBSArchitecture__system(self, target); }  pattern SystemAnalysis__system(self : SystemAnalysis, target : SystemComponent) { 	SystemAnalysis.ownedSystemComponentPkg(self, pckg); 	SystemComponentPkg.ownedSystemComponents(pckg, target); 	Component.actor(target, false); }  pattern LogicalArchitecture__system(self : LogicalArchitecture, target : LogicalComponent) { 	LogicalArchitecture.ownedLogicalComponentPkg(self, pckg); 	LogicalComponentPkg.ownedLogicalComponents(pckg, target); 	Component.actor(target, false); }  pattern PhysicalArchitecture__system(self : PhysicalArchitecture, target : PhysicalComponent) { 	PhysicalArchitecture.ownedPhysicalComponentPkg(self, pckg); 	PhysicalComponentPkg.ownedPhysicalComponents(pckg, target); 	Component.actor(target, false); }  pattern EPBSArchitecture__system(self : EPBSArchitecture, target : ConfigurationItem) { 	EPBSArchitecture.ownedConfigurationItemPkg(self, pckg); 	ConfigurationItemPkg.ownedConfigurationItems(pckg, target); 	Component.actor(target, false); }") |

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *Block*

A modular unit that describes the structure of a system or element.
[source: SysML specification v1.1]

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *ComponentArchitecture*

A specialized kind of BlockArchitecture, serving as a parent class for the various architecture levels, from System analysis down to EPBS architecture

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [Namespace](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namespace),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

Known Sub Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/SystemAnalysis.gif) [SystemAnalysis](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-ctx#-systemanalysis), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/EPBSArchitecture.gif) [EPBSArchitecture](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-epbs#-epbsarchitecture), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/LogicalArchitecture.gif) [LogicalArchitecture](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-la#-logicalarchitecture), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/PhysicalArchitecture.gif) [PhysicalArchitecture](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-pa#-physicalarchitecture)

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.debug.ui/icons/full/obj16/read_obj.png)](#system "pattern BlockArchitecture__system(self : BlockArchitecture, target : Component) { 	find SystemAnalysis__system(self, target); } or { 	find LogicalArchitecture__system(self, target); } or { 	find PhysicalArchitecture__system(self, target); } or { 	find EPBSArchitecture__system(self, target); }  pattern SystemAnalysis__system(self : SystemAnalysis, target : SystemComponent) { 	SystemAnalysis.ownedSystemComponentPkg(self, pckg); 	SystemComponentPkg.ownedSystemComponents(pckg, target); 	Component.actor(target, false); }  pattern LogicalArchitecture__system(self : LogicalArchitecture, target : LogicalComponent) { 	LogicalArchitecture.ownedLogicalComponentPkg(self, pckg); 	LogicalComponentPkg.ownedLogicalComponents(pckg, target); 	Component.actor(target, false); }  pattern PhysicalArchitecture__system(self : PhysicalArchitecture, target : PhysicalComponent) { 	PhysicalArchitecture.ownedPhysicalComponentPkg(self, pckg); 	PhysicalComponentPkg.ownedPhysicalComponents(pckg, target); 	Component.actor(target, false); }  pattern EPBSArchitecture__system(self : EPBSArchitecture, target : ConfigurationItem) { 	EPBSArchitecture.ownedConfigurationItemPkg(self, pckg); 	ConfigurationItemPkg.ownedConfigurationItems(pckg, target); 	Component.actor(target, false); }") |

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *Component*

An entity, with discrete structure within the system, that interacts with other Components of the system, thereby contributing at its lowest level to the system properties and characteristics.
[source: Sys EM , ISO/IEC CD 15288]

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [AbstractNamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-abstractnamedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [Namespace](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namespace),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [NamedElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-namedelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

Known Sub Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/SystemComponent.gif) [SystemComponent](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-ctx#-systemcomponent), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/ConfigurationItem.gif) [ConfigurationItem](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-epbs#-configurationitem), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/LogicalComponent.gif) [LogicalComponent](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-la#-logicalcomponent), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Entity.gif) [Entity](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-oa#-entity), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/PhysicalComponent.gif) [PhysicalComponent](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-pa#-physicalcomponent)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Part.gif) Part

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

In SysML, a Part is an owned property of a Block
[source: SysML glossary for SysML v1.0]

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) *ArchitectureAllocation*

Mediator class between BlockArchitecture elements, to represent an allocation link

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)

Known Sub Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/OperationalAnalysisRealization.gif) [OperationalAnalysisRealization](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-ctx#-operationalanalysisrealization), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/PhysicalArchitectureRealization.gif) [PhysicalArchitectureRealization](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-epbs#-physicalarchitecturerealization), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/SystemAnalysisRealization.gif) [SystemAnalysisRealization](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-la#-systemanalysisrealization), ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/LogicalArchitectureRealization.gif) [LogicalArchitectureRealization](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-pa#-logicalarchitecturerealization)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/Interface.gif) Interface

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

An interface is a kind of classifier that represents a declaration of a set of coherent public features and obligations. An
interface specifies a contract; any instance of a classifier that realizes the interface must fulfill that contract.
[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

Interfaces are defined by functional and physical characteristics that exist at a common boundary with co-functioning items and allow systems, equipment, software, and system data to be compatible.


That design feature of one piece of equipment that affects a design feature of another piece of equipment. 
An interface can extend beyond the physical boundary between two items. (For example, the weight and center of gravity of one item can affect the interfacing item; however, the center of gravity is rarely located at the physical boundary.
An electrical interface generally extends to the first isolating element rather than terminating at a series of connector pins.)

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/InterfaceImplementation.gif) InterfaceImplementation

Mediator class between an Interface and its implementor (typically a Component)


An InterfaceRealization is a specialized Realization relationship between a Classifier and an Interface. This relationship
signifies that the realizing classifier conforms to the contract specified by the Interface.
[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

## ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse/capella/master/core/plugins/org.polarsys.capella.core.data.res.edit/icons/full/obj16/PhysicalLinkEnd.gif) PhysicalLinkEnd

[![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/eclipse-platform/eclipse.platform.images/master/org.eclipse.images/eclipse-png/org.eclipse.jdt.ui/icons/full/obj16/annotation_obj.png)](# "semantic")

an endpoint of a physical link

A connector end is an endpoint of a connector, which attaches the connector to a connectable element. Each connector
end is part of one connector.
[source: UML superstructure v2.2]

Common Types: ![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [CapellaElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-capellacore#-capellaelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [TraceableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-traceableelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [ModelElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-modelelement),![](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/kchobantonov/org.eclipse.emf/master/plugins/org.eclipse.emf.ecore.edit/icons/full/obj16/EClass.gif) [PublishableElement](https://github.com/eclipse/capella/wiki/Metamodel-modellingcore#-publishableelement)
